		monastic_relic_tear = {
			destroy_artifact = shallya_tear_phial
			clr_global_flag = monastic_relic_tear_taken
		}
		monastic_relic_heart = {
			destroy_artifact = bleeding_heart_reliquary
			clr_global_flag = monastic_relic_heart_taken
		}
		monastic_relic_shroud = {
			destroy_artifact = pergunda_shroud
			clr_global_flag = monastic_relic_shroud_taken
		}
	}
	monastic_relic_clear_effect = yes
}

# Scope: the thief. Every member despises the thief, and the order casts
# them out for good.
# Used by: WHG_monastic_events
monastic_relic_cast_out_effect = {
	hidden_effect = {
		society = {
			any_society_member = {
				limit = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
				opinion = {
					who = ROOT
					modifier = stole_artifact
					years = 20
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { society_member_of = cult_of_shallya }
		leave_society = yes
		hidden_effect = { set_character_flag = betrayed_cult_of_shallya }
	}
}

# Scope: the thief, event_target:monastic_artifact_witness the witness.
# The fight with the witness: unharmed, wounded, maimed or dead
# (monastic.105 - 108), by the gap in combat rating.
# Used by: WHG_monastic_events
monastic_relic_fight_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			combat_rating_diff = { who = event_target:monastic_artifact_witness value = 50 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_great }
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				84 = { character_event = { id = monastic.105 } }
				10 = { character_event = { id = monastic.106 } }
				5 = { character_event = { id = monastic.107 } }
				1 = { character_event = { id = monastic.108 } }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			combat_rating_diff = { who = event_target:monastic_artifact_witness value = 30 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_very_good }
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				70 = { character_event = { id = monastic.105 } }
				15 = { character_event = { id = monastic.106 } }
				10 = { character_event = { id = monastic.107 } }
				5 = { character_event = { id = monastic.108 } }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			combat_rating_diff = { who = event_target:monastic_artifact_witness value = 10 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_good }
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				60 = { character_event = { id = monastic.105 } }
				20 = { character_event = { id = monastic.106 } }
				15 = { character_event = { id = monastic.107 } }
				5 = { character_event = { id = monastic.108 } }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				event_target:monastic_artifact_witness = {
					combat_rating_diff = { who = ROOT value = 10 }
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_even }
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				20 = { character_event = { id = monastic.105 } }
				20 = { character_event = { id = monastic.106 } }
				20 = { character_event = { id = monastic.107 } }
				40 = { character_event = { id = monastic.108 } }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				event_target:monastic_artifact_witness = {
					combat_rating_diff = { who = ROOT value = 30 }
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_poor }
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				5 = { character_event = { id = monastic.105 } }
				15 = { character_event = { id = monastic.106 } }
				20 = { character_event = { id = monastic.107 } }
				60 = { character_event = { id = monastic.108 } }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				event_target:monastic_artifact_witness = {
					combat_rating_diff = { who = ROOT value = 50 }
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_very_poor }
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				1 = { character_event = { id = monastic.105 } }
				4 = { character_event = { id = monastic.106 } }
				15 = { character_event = { id = monastic.107 } }
				80 = { character_event = { id = monastic.108 } }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_terrible }
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				1 = { character_event = { id = monastic.106 } }
				9 = { character_event = { id = monastic.107 } }
				90 = { character_event = { id = monastic.108 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Scope: the thief. Finds a greedy or deceitful member who may piece the
# murder together.
# Used by: WHG_monastic_events
monastic_relic_blackmailer_effect = {
	society = {
		random_society_member = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				is_society_grandmaster = no
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_friend = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_lover = ROOT }
				NOT = { character = event_target:monastic_artifact_witness }
				OR = {
					trait = greedy
					trait = deceitful
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = monastic_artifact_blackmailer
		}
	}
}

# Scope: the guardian, FROM the ward. One to three points of the skill the
# guardian picked (monastic_order_education_* flags), if any.
# Used by: WHG_monastic_events
monastic_ward_skill_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = monastic_order_education_diplomacy
				has_character_flag = monastic_order_education_martial
				has_character_flag = monastic_order_education_stewardship
				has_character_flag = monastic_order_education_intrigue
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			30 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.9
					is_learned_trigger = yes
				}
				trigger_switch = {
					on_trigger = has_character_flag
					monastic_order_education_diplomacy = { FROM = { change_diplomacy = 1 } }
					monastic_order_education_martial = { FROM = { change_martial = 1 } }
					monastic_order_education_stewardship = { FROM = { change_stewardship = 1 } }
					monastic_order_education_intrigue = { FROM = { change_intrigue = 1 } }
				}
			}
			50 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					FROM = { is_dumb_trigger = yes }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					is_learned_trigger = yes
				}
				trigger_switch = {
					on_trigger = has_character_flag
					monastic_order_education_diplomacy = { FROM = { change_diplomacy = 2 } }
					monastic_order_education_martial = { FROM = { change_martial = 2 } }
					monastic_order_education_stewardship = { FROM = { change_stewardship = 2 } }
					monastic_order_education_intrigue = { FROM = { change_intrigue = 2 } }
				}
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					FROM = { is_dumb_trigger = yes }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					FROM = { is_smart_trigger = yes }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					is_learned_trigger = yes
				}
				trigger_switch = {
					on_trigger = has_character_flag
					monastic_order_education_diplomacy = { FROM = { change_diplomacy = 3 } }
					monastic_order_education_martial = { FROM = { change_martial = 3 } }
					monastic_order_education_stewardship = { FROM = { change_stewardship = 3 } }
					monastic_order_education_intrigue = { FROM = { change_intrigue = 3 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Scope: the former ward, ROOT the guardian. Picks a sin to give up or a
# virtue to take; the guardian's rank decides how far the advice can go.
# Used by: WHG_monastic_events
monastic_ward_pick_virtue_effect = {
	random_list = {
		10 = {
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { trait = lustful }
			}
			set_character_flag = monastic_order_education_remove_lustful
		}
		10 = {
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { trait = gluttonous }
			}
			set_character_flag = monastic_order_education_remove_gluttonous
		}
		10 = {
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { trait = greedy }
			}
			set_character_flag = monastic_order_education_remove_greedy
		}
		10 = {
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { trait = slothful }
			}
			set_character_flag = monastic_order_education_remove_slothful
		}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { trait = envious }

			modifier = {
				factor = 0

			}
			set_character_flag = monastic_order_education_remove_wroth

					NOT = { trait = ambitious }

			set_character_flag = monastic_order_education_add_humble

					trait = charitable

				}
			}
			set_character_flag = monastic_order_education_add_charitable
		}
		10 = {
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					trait = chaste
					trait = lustful
					ROOT = { NOT = { society_rank >= 2 } }
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = monastic_order_education_add_chaste
		}
		10 = {
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					trait = kind
					trait = cruel
					ROOT = { NOT = { society_rank >= 2 } }
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = monastic_order_education_add_kind
		}
		10 = {
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					trait = temperate
					trait = gluttonous
					ROOT = { NOT = { society_rank >= 2 } }
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = monastic_order_education_add_temperate

		10 = {

# Scope: the former ward. Takes the advice that was picked.
# Used by: WHG_monastic_events
monastic_ward_virtue_effect = {
	trigger_switch = {
		on_trigger = has_character_flag
		monastic_order_education_remove_lustful = { remove_trait = lustful }
		monastic_order_education_remove_gluttonous = { remove_trait = gluttonous }
		monastic_order_education_remove_greedy = { remove_trait = greedy }
		monastic_order_education_remove_slothful = { remove_trait = slothful }
		monastic_order_education_remove_envious = { remove_trait = envious }
		monastic_order_education_remove_wroth = { remove_trait = wroth }
		monastic_order_education_remove_proud = { remove_trait = proud }
		monastic_order_education_remove_deceitful = { remove_trait = deceitful }
		monastic_order_education_remove_cruel = { remove_trait = cruel }
		monastic_order_education_remove_cynical = { remove_trait = cynical }
		monastic_order_education_remove_ambitious = { remove_trait = ambitious }
		monastic_order_education_add_humble = { add_trait = humble }
		monastic_order_education_add_charitable = { add_trait = charitable }
		monastic_order_education_add_chaste = { add_trait = chaste }
		monastic_order_education_add_kind = { add_trait = kind }
		monastic_order_education_add_temperate = { add_trait = temperate }
		monastic_order_education_add_patient = { add_trait = patient }
		monastic_order_education_add_diligent = { add_trait = diligent }
		monastic_order_education_add_honest = { add_trait = honest }
		monastic_order_education_add_content = { add_trait = content }
	}
}

# Used by WHG_northern_rites_events

# How the fight ends once the winner is known: the loser flees, yields or
# dies, and the winner may carry a scar from it. Sets the outcome flags the
# result events read: holmgang_won or holmgang_lost, one of holmgang_kill,
# holmgang_yield or holmgang_flight on both, holmgang_scarred on the winner
# and holmgang_maimed on a loser who yields.
# A craven may flee (never a berserker). A berserker or a man of the Blood
# God kills more often, a kind one spares more often, and a much weaker foe
# dies more often and leaves fewer scars.
# Scope: any; needs the event targets holmgang_winner and holmgang_loser.
# Used by: WHG_northern_rites_events
holmgang_outcome_effect = {
	event_target:holmgang_winner = { set_character_flag = holmgang_won }
	event_target:holmgang_loser = { set_character_flag = holmgang_lost }
	event_target:holmgang_winner = {
		random_list = {
			40 = { # The loser runs for the boats
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					event_target:holmgang_loser = {
						OR = {
							NOT = { trait = craven }
							trait = berserker
						}
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = holmgang_flight
				event_target:holmgang_loser = { set_character_flag = holmgang_flight }
			}
			20 = { # The loser dies on the holm
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					event_target:holmgang_loser = {
						trait = craven
						NOT = { trait = berserker }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					OR = {
						trait = berserker
						religion_khorne_worshipper_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					trait = wroth
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					trait = kind
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					event_target:holmgang_loser = {
						OR = {
							is_the_worst_dueler_compared_to_prev_trigger = yes
							is_terrible_dueler_compared_to_prev_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = holmgang_kill
				event_target:holmgang_loser = { set_character_flag = holmgang_kill }
			}
			60 = { # The loser yields
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.67
					event_target:holmgang_loser = {
						trait = craven
						NOT = { trait = berserker }
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = holmgang_yield
				event_target:holmgang_loser = { set_character_flag = holmgang_yield }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = holmgang_flight } }
			random_list = {
				60 = {}
				40 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						event_target:holmgang_loser = {
							OR = {
								is_the_worst_dueler_compared_to_prev_trigger = yes
								is_terrible_dueler_compared_to_prev_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5
						event_target:holmgang_loser = {
							OR = {
								is_good_dueler_compared_to_prev_trigger = yes
								is_great_dueler_compared_to_prev_trigger = yes
								is_fantastic_dueler_compared_to_prev_trigger = yes
								is_the_best_dueler_compared_to_prev_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = holmgang_scarred
				}
			}
		}
	}
	event_target:holmgang_loser = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = holmgang_yield }
			random = {
				chance = 30
				set_character_flag = holmgang_maimed
			}
		}
	}
}

# Clears every holmgang flag. Run for both fighters when it is over, and by
# the safety event holmgang.90.
# Scope: a fighter.
# Used by: WHG_northern_rites_events, WHG_faith_effects
holmgang_clear_flags_effect = {
	clr_character_flag = holmgang_challenger
	clr_character_flag = holmgang_defender
	clr_character_flag = holmgang_won
	clr_character_flag = holmgang_lost
	clr_character_flag = holmgang_drew
	clr_character_flag = holmgang_kill
	clr_character_flag = holmgang_yield
	clr_character_flag = holmgang_flight
	clr_character_flag = holmgang_scarred
	clr_character_flag = holmgang_maimed
	clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
}

# The chances shown before the fight, from the foe's skill against PREV's
# (the same bands holmgang.11 weighs the fight on).
# Scope: the foe; PREV is the fighter who reads the tooltip.
# Used by: WHG_northern_rites_events
holmgang_foe_chances_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_the_worst_dueler_compared_to_prev_trigger = yes }
		custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_great }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_terrible_dueler_compared_to_prev_trigger = yes }
		custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_very_good }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_bad_dueler_compared_to_prev_trigger = yes }
		custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_good }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				is_ok_dueler_compared_to_prev_trigger = yes
				is_decent_dueler_compared_to_prev_trigger = yes
				is_good_dueler_compared_to_prev_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_even }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_great_dueler_compared_to_prev_trigger = yes }
		custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_poor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_fantastic_dueler_compared_to_prev_trigger = yes }
		custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_very_poor }
	}
	else = {
		custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_terrible }
	}
}

# The loser dies on the holm: the winner takes a fifth of the dead man's
# wealth by holm law, then death (the invincible cheat it).
# Scope: the loser; needs the event target holmgang_winner.
# Used by: WHG_northern_rites_events
holmgang_death_effect = {
	holmgang_clear_flags_effect = yes
	transfer_scaled_wealth = {
		to = event_target:holmgang_winner
		value = 0.2
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
		antideath_effect = yes
	}
	else = {
		death = {
			death_reason = death_duel
			killer = event_target:holmgang_winner
		}
	}
}

# Sorts the prisoner brought to the blood-tree into one kind, kept as a flag
# on the host for the desc and option variants of rites.12 and rites.13:
# kin first (rites_victim_kin plus the bond), then the head of a faith, then
# dwarf, elf, greenskin, lizardman, a servant of the Dark Gods (only for a
# host who does not serve them), a faithful of the Old World gods (all of
# these also rites_victim_foreign), else a common captive in one of four
# moods.
# Scope: the host; FROM is the prisoner.
# Used by: WHG_northern_rites_events
rites_blot_classify_victim_effect = {
	rites_blot_clear_victim_flags_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				sibling = FROM
				father = { character = FROM }
				mother = { character = FROM }
				FROM = { father = { character = ROOT } }
				FROM = { mother = { character = ROOT } }
				any_spouse = { character = FROM }
				any_dynasty_member = { character = FROM }
			}
		}
		set_character_flag = rites_victim_kin
		if = {
			limit = { sibling = FROM FROM = { is_woman_trigger = no } }
			set_character_flag = rites_victim_brother
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { sibling = FROM }
			set_character_flag = rites_victim_sister
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { father = { character = FROM } }
			set_character_flag = rites_victim_father
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { mother = { character = FROM } }
			set_character_flag = rites_victim_mother
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					OR = {
						father = { character = ROOT }
						mother = { character = ROOT }
					}
					is_woman_trigger = no
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = rites_victim_son
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					OR = {
						father = { character = ROOT }
						mother = { character = ROOT }
					}
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = rites_victim_daughter
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { any_spouse = { character = FROM } }
			clr_character_flag = rites_victim_kin # A spouse is no blood kin
			set_character_flag = rites_victim_spouse
		}
		else = {
			set_character_flag = rites_victim_kinsman
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { FROM = { controls_religion = yes } }
		set_character_flag = rites_victim_foreign
		set_character_flag = rites_victim_head
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { FROM = { culture_group = dwarf_group } }
		set_character_flag = rites_victim_foreign
		set_character_flag = rites_victim_dwarf
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { FROM = { is_elf = yes } }
		set_character_flag = rites_victim_foreign
		set_character_flag = rites_victim_elf
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					culture_group = orc_group
					culture_group = goblin_group
				}
			}
		}
		set_character_flag = rites_victim_foreign
		set_character_flag = rites_victim_greenskin
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { FROM = { culture_group = lizardman_group } }
		set_character_flag = rites_victim_foreign
		set_character_flag = rites_victim_lizardman
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = { religion_group = chaos_gods_group }
			NOT = { religion_group = chaos_gods_group }
		}
		set_character_flag = rites_victim_foreign
		set_character_flag = rites_victim_chaos
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { FROM = { religion_group = old_world_group } }
		set_character_flag = rites_victim_foreign
		set_character_flag = rites_victim_old_world
	}
	else = {
		set_character_flag = rites_victim_common
		random_list = {
			25 = { set_character_flag = rites_victim_mood_piss }
			25 = { set_character_flag = rites_victim_mood_run }
			25 = { set_character_flag = rites_victim_mood_calm }
			25 = { set_character_flag = rites_victim_mood_spit }
		}
	}
}

# The prisoner hangs from the blood-tree. A common captive is worth 25
# prestige and 15 piety, a foreign one 50 and 25, the head of a faith 250 and
# 100 with a blow to that faith. A man of the Blood God gains 25 piety more.
# Kin make the host a kinslayer. The guests see it (rites.13), then the next
# prisoner is fetched (rites.10).
# Scope: the host; FROM is the prisoner.
# Used by: WHG_northern_rites_events
rites_blot_offer_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { controls_religion = yes } }
		prestige = 250
		piety = 100
		religion_authority = { modifier = sacrificed_head_good years = 20 }
		FROM = { religion_authority = { modifier = sacrificed_head_bad years = 20 } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
				NOT = { has_character_flag = achievement_holy_smoke }
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_character_flag = achievement_holy_smoke }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = rites_victim_foreign }
		prestige = 50
		piety = 25
	}
	else = {
		prestige = 25
		piety = 15
	}
	if = {
		limit = { religion_khorne_worshipper_trigger = yes }
		piety = 25
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = rites_victim_kin }
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = kinslayer_target }
		add_kinslayer_trait_effect = yes
	}
	change_variable = { which = blot_offerings value = 1 }
	hidden_effect = {
		any_vassal = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = rites_blot_guest }
			character_event = { id = rites.13 }
		}
	}
	FROM = {
		clr_character_flag = rites_blot_victim
		if = {
			limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
			antideath_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			death = {
				death_reason = death_sacrificed_norse
				killer = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = rites.10 days = 2 } }
}

# The host stays the hangman's hand: piety is lost (more for a foreign
# captive), the prisoner goes back to the dungeon grateful (rites.14), and
# the next prisoner is fetched (rites.10).
# Scope: the host; FROM is the prisoner.
# Used by: WHG_northern_rites_events
rites_blot_spare_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = rites_victim_foreign }
		piety = -50
	}
	else = {
		piety = -25
	}
	set_character_flag = rites_blot_spared_one
	FROM = {
		clr_character_flag = rites_blot_victim
		character_event = { id = rites.14 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPrites14 }
	}
	hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = rites.10 days = 2 } }
}

# Clears every blot flag and the offering count, then the feast flags.
# Scope: the host or a guest.
# Used by: WHG_northern_rites_events
rites_blot_clear_flags_effect = {
	clr_character_flag = holding_blot
	clr_character_flag = rites_blot_guest
	clr_character_flag = rites_blot_paid
	clr_character_flag = rites_blot_victim
	clr_character_flag = rites_blot_spared_one
	clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
	rites_blot_clear_victim_flags_effect = yes
	set_variable = { which = blot_offerings value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = blot_fetched value = 0 }
	rites_feast_clear_flags_effect = yes
}

# The host and the other guests see the scene (rites.59); the culprit and
# partner are left out.
# Scope: the culprit; event targets rites_feast_host, rites_feast_culprit and
#   rites_feast_partner (the culprit again when the scene has no partner).
# Used by: WHG_northern_rites_events
rites_feast_hall_sees_effect = {
	custom_tooltip = { text = rites_feast_hall_sees_tt }
	hidden_effect = {
		event_target:rites_feast_host = {
			character_event = { id = rites.59 }
			any_vassal = {
				limit = {
					rites_feast_guest_trigger = yes
					NOT = { character = event_target:rites_feast_culprit }
					NOT = { character = event_target:rites_feast_partner }
				}
				character_event = { id = rites.59 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# The two guests at a feast become lovers (never in races without women).
# Scope: either lover; event targets rites_feast_culprit, rites_feast_partner.
# Used by: WHG_northern_rites_events
rites_feast_take_lover_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { character = event_target:rites_feast_culprit }
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_no_females_race = no
				event_target:rites_feast_partner = { is_no_females_race = no }
			}
			add_lover = event_target:rites_feast_partner
		}
	}
	else = {
		tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_no_females_race = no
					event_target:rites_feast_culprit = { is_no_females_race = no }
				}
				add_lover = event_target:rites_feast_culprit
			}
		}
	}
}

# The chosen guest rides as Dazh: the host and the other guests who are no
# cynics think better of him for a year, and all of them see it (rites.105).
# Scope: the rider; FROM is the host.
# Used by: WHG_northern_rites_events
rites_dazh_rider_effect = {
	FROM = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = cynical } }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_represented_jarilo who = ROOT }
		}
		any_vassal = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = rites_dazh_guest
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { trait = cynical }
			}
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_represented_jarilo who = ROOT }
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = rites.105 }
			any_vassal = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = rites_dazh_guest
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
				}
				character_event = { id = rites.105 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Clears every flag of the festival of Dazh, then the feast flags.
# Scope: the host or a guest.
# Used by: WHG_northern_rites_events
rites_dazh_clear_flags_effect = {
	clr_character_flag = holding_jarilo
	clr_character_flag = rites_dazh_guest
	clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
	rites_feast_clear_flags_effect = yes
}

# How the dead parent died, as a flag on ROOT for the inscription
# (runestone.10): by ROOT's own hand first (murder, execution, dungeon or
# sacrifice), then natural, battle, executed, murdered, accident (and murder
# by an unknown hand), dungeon, sacrificed, duel, suicide, else other.
# Scope: the commissioner; event target runestone_parent.
# Used by: WHG_northern_rites_events
runestone_parent_death_effect = {
	event_target:runestone_parent = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				killer = { character = ROOT }
				OR = {
					death_murder_known_trigger = yes
					death_execution_trigger = yes
					death_reason = death_dungeon
					death_sacrificed_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			ROOT = { set_character_flag = runestone_death_by_you }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { death_reason = death_natural }
			ROOT = { set_character_flag = runestone_death_natural }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { death_reason = death_battle }
			ROOT = { set_character_flag = runestone_death_battle }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { death_execution_trigger = yes }
			ROOT = { set_character_flag = runestone_death_executed }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { death_murder_known_trigger = yes }
			ROOT = { set_character_flag = runestone_death_murdered }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					death_accident_trigger = yes
					death_murder_unknown_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			ROOT = { set_character_flag = runestone_death_accident }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { death_reason = death_dungeon }
			ROOT = { set_character_flag = runestone_death_dungeon }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { death_sacrificed_trigger = yes }
			ROOT = { set_character_flag = runestone_death_sacrificed }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { death_reason = death_duel }
			ROOT = { set_character_flag = runestone_death_duel }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { death_suicide_trigger = yes }
			ROOT = { set_character_flag = runestone_death_suicide }
		}
		else = {
			ROOT = { set_character_flag = runestone_death_other }
		}
	}
}

# The reward for a raised stone: 200 prestige and 100 piety for most stones;
# 300 prestige for a stone of beauty, wisdom, strength, pride, courage, raids
# or a parent who fell in battle (250 for a duel); only 100 prestige for a
# mad, possessed or witless boast, a parent's suicide or a parent ROOT killed.
# Then two years of raised_runestone and a chronicle entry.
# Scope: the commissioner.
# Used by: WHG_northern_rites_events
runestone_reward_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = runestone_self_fair
				has_character_flag = runestone_self_genius
				has_character_flag = runestone_self_strong
				has_character_flag = runestone_self_proud
				has_character_flag = runestone_self_brave
				has_character_flag = runestone_self_raider
				has_character_flag = runestone_death_battle
			}
		}
		prestige = 300
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_death_duel }
		prestige = 250
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = runestone_self_lunatic
				has_character_flag = runestone_self_possessed
				has_character_flag = runestone_self_imbecile
				has_character_flag = runestone_death_suicide
				has_character_flag = runestone_death_by_you
			}
		}
		prestige = 100
	}
	else = {
		prestige = 200
		piety = 100
	}
	add_character_modifier = {
		name = raised_runestone
		duration = 730
	}
	if = {
		limit = { ai = no }
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_for_father }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_father picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_for_mother }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_mother picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_lunatic }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_lunatic picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_drunkard }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_drunkard picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_fair }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_fair picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_dwarf }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_dwarf picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_genius }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_genius picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_imbecile }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_imbecile picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_strong }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_strong picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_scholar }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_scholar picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_impaler }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_impaler picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_poet }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_poet picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_lustful }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_lustful picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_gluttonous }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_gluttonous picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_charitable }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_charitable picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_kind }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_kind picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_proud }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_proud picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_humble }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_humble picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_brave }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_brave picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_just }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_just picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_zealous_old }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_zealous_old picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_zealous }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_zealous_other picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_cruel }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_cruel picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_possessed }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_possessed picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_grudge }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_grudge picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = runestone_self_raider }
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_raider picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
		else = {
			chronicle = { entry = runestone_chronicle_generic picture = GFX_evt_runestone_raising_oldgods }
		}
	}
	runestone_clear_flags_effect = yes
}

}

# Starts the patron's sign loop (patron.1) for a playable follower, once.
# Scope: the follower.
# Used by: WHG_patron_god_events
patron_start_signs_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_playable = yes
			NOT = { has_character_flag = patron_signs_running }
		}
		set_character_flag = patron_signs_running
		character_event = { id = patron.1 days = 365 random = 365 }
	}
}

# Takes away the patron god (and the blessings of the vanilla pantheons
# whose followers may still carry them).
# Scope: the follower.
# Used by: WHG_patron_god_events
patron_remove_god_effect = {
	remove_character_modifier = follower_of_grungni
	remove_character_modifier = follower_of_grimnir
	remove_character_modifier = follower_of_valaya
	remove_character_modifier = follower_of_thungni
	remove_character_modifier = follower_of_ursun
	remove_character_modifier = follower_of_dazh
	remove_character_modifier = follower_of_tor
	remove_character_modifier = follower_of_kalita
	remove_character_modifier = follower_of_ptra
	remove_character_modifier = follower_of_asaph
	remove_character_modifier = follower_of_phakth
	remove_character_modifier = follower_of_usirian
	remove_character_modifier = follower_of_dievas
	remove_character_modifier = follower_of_velnias
	remove_character_modifier = follower_of_perkunas
	remove_character_modifier = follower_of_zemyna
	remove_character_modifier = voice_of_dievas
	remove_character_modifier = voice_of_akka
	remove_character_modifier = zemynas_blessing
	remove_character_modifier = yemojas_blessing
	remove_character_modifier = akkas_blessing
}

# The god's trial: Tor frightens a timid child, spouse or lover with thunder;
# Asaph kindles passion for a courtier or vassal; Valaya, Ptra and Ursun bless
# a marriage; Usirian sends a child with questions about death; the others
# send a sign of their own (patron.37).
# Scope: the follower.
# Used by: WHG_patron_god_events
patron_trial_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_modifier = follower_of_tor }
		random_spouse = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				NOT = { trait = brave }
				NOT = { has_character_flag = scared_of_thunder }
			}
			save_event_target_as = patron_thunder_target
		}
		random_lover = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				NOT = { trait = brave }
				NOT = { has_character_flag = scared_of_thunder }
			}
			save_event_target_as = patron_thunder_target
		}
		random_child = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = no
				NOT = { trait = brave }
				NOT = { trait = willful }
				NOT = { has_character_flag = scared_of_thunder }
			}
			save_event_target_as = patron_thunder_target
		}
		event_target:patron_thunder_target = { character_event = { id = patron.23 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_modifier = follower_of_asaph }
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				is_adult = yes
				NOT = { is_close_relative = ROOT }
				root_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
			save_event_target_as = patron_lover
		}
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				is_adult = yes
				NOT = { is_close_relative = ROOT }
				root_attraction_trigger = yes
				OR = {
					trait = lustful
					trait = fair
					has_character_modifier = follower_of_asaph
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = patron_lover
		}
		event_target:patron_lover = { character_event = { id = patron.25 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_valaya
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_ptra
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_ursun
			}
			OR = {
				is_female = no
				AND = {
					is_woman_trigger = yes
					NOT = { age = 46 }
				}
			}
			NOR = {
				trait = celibate
				trait = eunuch
			}
		}
		random_spouse = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_female = no
					AND = {
						is_woman_trigger = yes
						NOT = { age = 46 }
					}
				}
				NOR = {
					trait = celibate
					trait = eunuch
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = patron.27 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_modifier = follower_of_usirian }
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				age = 4
				is_adult = no
				NOT = { has_character_flag = asked_about_death }
			}
			save_event_target_as = patron_death_child
		}
		random_child = {
			limit = {
				age = 4
				is_adult = no
				NOT = { has_character_flag = asked_about_death }
			}
			save_event_target_as = patron_death_child
		}
		event_target:patron_death_child = { character_event = { id = patron.29 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_grimnir
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_grungni
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_thungni
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_dazh
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_kalita
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_phakth
			}
		}
		character_event = { id = patron.37 }
	}
}

# Renown in the god's field: warriors, a name as the god's voice, a rich
# year, an overheard plot or new insights.
# Scope: the follower.
# Used by: WHG_patron_god_events
patron_renown_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_grimnir
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_tor
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_phakth
			}
		}
		character_event = { id = patron.32 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_valaya
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_ursun
			}
		}
		character_event = { id = patron.33 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_grungni
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_dazh
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_ptra
			}
		}
		character_event = { id = patron.34 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_kalita
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_usirian
			}
		}
		character_event = { id = patron.35 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_thungni
				has_character_modifier = follower_of_asaph
			}
		}
		character_event = { id = patron.36 }
	}
}

# Takes Sigmar as patron: the blessing, piety for one of the god's own faith, a
# chronicle line for players and the start of the patron's favour.
# Scope: the follower.
# Used by: WHG_patron_god_events
patron_god_take_sigmar_effect = {
	add_character_modifier = { name = patron_god_sigmar duration = -1 }
	if = {
		limit = { religion = sigmarite }
		piety = 50
	}
	clr_character_flag = selecting_patron_deity
	if = {
		limit = { ai = no }
		chronicle = {
			entry = CHRONICLE_PATRON_GOD_SIGMAR
			picture = GFX_evt_priest_blessing
		}
	}
	patron_god_start_favour_effect = yes
}

# Takes Taal and Rhya as patron: the blessing, piety for one of the god's own
# faith, a
# chronicle line for players and the start of the patron's favour.
# Scope: the follower.
# Used by: WHG_patron_god_events
patron_god_take_taal_effect = {
	add_character_modifier = { name = patron_god_taal duration = -1 }
	if = {
		limit = { religion = taalite }
		piety = 50
	}
	clr_character_flag = selecting_patron_deity
	if = {
		limit = { ai = no }
		chronicle = {
			entry = CHRONICLE_PATRON_GOD_TAAL

		}
	}
	patron_god_start_favour_effect = yes
}

# Takes Myrmidia as patron: the blessing, piety for one of the god's own faith,
# a
# chronicle line for players and the start of the patron's favour.
# Scope: the follower.
# Used by: WHG_patron_god_events
patron_god_take_myrmidia_effect = {
	add_character_modifier = { name = patron_god_myrmidia duration = -1 }
	if = {
		limit = { religion = myrmidian }
		piety = 50
	}
	clr_character_flag = selecting_patron_deity
	if = {
		limit = { ai = no }
		chronicle = {
			entry = CHRONICLE_PATRON_GOD_MYRMIDIA
			picture = GFX_evt_priest_blessing
		}
	}
	patron_god_start_favour_effect = yes
}

# Creates the chieftain of a rising clan, of the county's own people. Where
# the county follows the Dark Gods (beastmen aside) the chieftain is sworn to
# one of them, most likely the county's own, and now and then is already a
# champion. The new character is the last one created.
# Scope: the county (province).
# Used by: WHG_revolt_events
revolt_clan_leader_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			religion_group = chaos_gods_group
			NOT = { culture_group = beastman_group }
		}
		random_list = {
			15 = { # A warlord of Chaos Undivided
				modifier = { factor = 4 religion = chaos }
				create_character = {
					random_traits = yes
					dynasty = random
					religion = chaos
					culture = ROOT
					female = no
					age = 20
					health = 7
					fertility = 0.7
					attributes = { martial = 8 diplomacy = 9 }
					trait = brave
					trait = ambitious
					trait = barbarian_3
				}
			}
			1 = { # A champion of Chaos Undivided
				modifier = { factor = 4 religion = chaos }
				create_character = {
					random_traits = yes
					dynasty = random
					religion = chaos
					culture = ROOT
					female = no
					age = 20
					health = 7
					fertility = 0.7
					attributes = { martial = 5 diplomacy = 3 stewardship = 2 learning = 3 intrigue = 3 }
					trait = brave
					trait = ambitious
					trait = champion_undivided
					trait = barbarian_4
				}
			}
			15 = { # A warlord of khorne
				modifier = { factor = 4 religion = khorne }
				create_character = {
					random_traits = yes
					dynasty = random
					religion = khorne
					culture = ROOT
					female = no
					age = 20
					health = 7
					fertility = 0.7
					attributes = { martial = 13 }
					trait = brave
					trait = wroth
					trait = barbarian_4
				}
			}
			1 = { # A champion of khorne
				modifier = { factor = 4 religion = khorne }
				create_character = {
					random_traits = yes
					dynasty = random
					religion = khorne
					culture = ROOT
					female = no
					age = 20
					health = 7
					fertility = 0.7
					attributes = { martial = 13 }
					trait = brave
					trait = wroth
					trait = champion_khorne
					trait = barbarian_4
				}
			}
			15 = { # A warlord of tzeentch
				modifier = { factor = 4 religion = tzeentch }
				create_character = {
					random_traits = yes
					dynasty = random
					religion = tzeentch
					culture = ROOT
					female = no
					age = 20
					health = 7
					fertility = 0.7
					attributes = { intrigue = 7 learning = 10 }
					trait = deceitful
					trait = ambitious
					trait = elusive_shadow
				}
			}
			1 = { # A champion of tzeentch
				modifier = { factor = 4 religion = tzeentch }
				create_character = {
					random_traits = yes
					dynasty = random
					religion = tzeentch
					culture = ROOT
					female = no
					age = 20
					health = 7
					fertility = 0.7
					attributes = { intrigue = 13 learning = 13 }
					trait = deceitful
					trait = ambitious
					trait = champion_tzeentch
					trait = elusive_shadow
				}
			}
			15 = { # A warlord of slaanesh
				modifier = { factor = 4 religion = slaanesh }
				create_character = {
					random_traits = yes
					dynasty = random
					religion = slaanesh
					culture = ROOT
					female = no
					age = 20
					health = 7
					fertility = 0.7
					attributes = { intrigue = 8 diplomacy = 9 }
					trait = lustful
					trait = proud
					trait = grey_eminence
				}
			}
			1 = { # A champion of slaanesh
				modifier = { factor = 4 religion = slaanesh }
				create_character = {
					random_traits = yes
					dynasty = random
					religion = slaanesh
					culture = ROOT
					female = no
					age = 20
					health = 7
					fertility = 0.7
					attributes = { intrigue = 8 diplomacy = 9 }
					trait = lustful
					trait = proud
					trait = champion_slaanesh
					trait = grey_eminence
				}
			}
			15 = { # A warlord of nurgle
				modifier = { factor = 4 religion = nurgle }
				create_character = {
					random_traits = yes
					dynasty = random
					religion = nurgle
					culture = ROOT
					female = no
					age = 20
					health = 7
					fertility = 0.7
					attributes = { diplomacy = 7 stewardship = 10 }
					trait = charitable
					trait = kind
					trait = has_nurgles_rot
					trait = midas_touched
				}
			}
			1 = { # A champion of nurgle
				modifier = { factor = 4 religion = nurgle }
				create_character = {
					random_traits = yes
					dynasty = random
					religion = nurgle
					culture = ROOT
					female = no
					age = 20
					health = 7
					fertility = 0.7
					attributes = { diplomacy = 7 stewardship = 10 }
					trait = charitable
					trait = kind
					trait = has_nurgles_rot
					trait = champion_nurgle
					trait = midas_touched
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_character = {
			random_traits = yes
			dynasty = random
			religion = ROOT
			culture = ROOT
			female = no
			age = 20
			health = 7
			fertility = 0.7
			attributes = { martial = 8 diplomacy = 9 }
			trait = brave
			trait = ambitious
			trait = barbarian_3
		}
	}

# Picks the size of the clan's bands (one third each) and raises three: the
# chieftain's own and those of two kinsmen at the chieftain's side.
# Scope: the clan chieftain; ROOT is the county.
# Used by: WHG_revolt_events
revolt_clan_bands_effect = {
	random_list = {
		34 = { set_character_flag = revolt_band_large }
		33 = { set_character_flag = revolt_band_medium }
		33 = { set_character_flag = revolt_band_small }
	}
	revolt_clan_band_effect = yes
	revolt_clan_band_effect = yes
	revolt_clan_band_effect = yes
	clr_character_flag = revolt_band_large
	clr_character_flag = revolt_band_medium
	clr_character_flag = revolt_band_small
}

# Ends a pilgrimage and clears everything it sets.
# Scope: the pilgrim.
# Used by: WHG_saint_events, WHG_old_world_faith_events
pilgrim_clear_effect = {
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_trait = on_pilgrimage
		clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
		clr_character_flag = preparing_pilgrimage
		clr_character_flag = activated_pilgrimage
		clr_character_flag = good_pilgrimage
		clr_character_flag = bad_pilgrimage
		clr_character_flag = abominable_pilgrimage
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_flame_blessed
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_flame_burned
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_to_averheim
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_to_middenheim
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_to_remas
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_to_magritta
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_to_krugenheim
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_to_talabheim
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_to_couronne
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_to_bordeleaux
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_to_luccini
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_to_isle_of_flame
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_to_gaen_vale
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_to_oak_of_ages
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_to_har_ganeth
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_to_karaz_a_karak
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_to_karak_kadrin
		clr_character_flag = pilgrim_to_kraka_drak
	}
}

# Saves a holy order of the pilgrim's faith as event target pilgrim_order
# (pilgrim.27 - 29).
# Scope: the pilgrim.
# Used by: WHG_saint_events
pilgrim_save_holy_order_effect = {
	random_independent_ruler = {
		limit = {
			holy_order = yes
			religion = ROOT
		}
		save_event_target_as = pilgrim_order
	}
}

# The roll at death that decides whether the dead may one day be raised to
# a Venerated Soul. If it passes, the case comes before the head of the
# cult three to thirty years later (one to three for a former player).
# Scope: the dead character (saint.1).
# Used by: WHG_saint_events
saint_canonization_roll_effect = {
	random = {
		chance = 1
		modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { rightful_religious_head_scope = { always = yes } } }
		modifier = {
			factor = 0
			NOR = { has_character_flag = beatified has_religion_feature = religion_beatification }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = cynical }
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.4
			OR = {
				has_landed_title = k_cult_of_sigmar
				has_landed_title = k_cult_of_ulric
				has_landed_title = k_cult_of_taal_and_rhya
				has_landed_title = k_cult_of_morr
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_any_opinion_modifier = opinion_baptism_sponsor_target }
		modifier = {
			factor = 2 # Players without a holy bloodline
			ai = no
			NOT = { any_owned_bloodline = { has_bloodline_flag = saintly_bloodline } }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.2 # The blood of a Venerated Soul already
			any_owned_bloodline = { has_bloodline_flag = saintly_bloodline }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = patient }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = diligent }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = kind }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = chaste }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = charitable }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = temperate }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = humble }
		modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = just }
		modifier = { factor = 1.2 has_crusade_trait_trigger = yes }
		modifier = {
			factor = 2 # Martyrs above all
			martyrs_death_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 piety = 1000 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 piety = 2000 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 piety = 3500 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 piety = 5000 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 piety = 7500 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 piety = 10000 }
		modifier = {
			factor = 3 # The first of a faith comes easier
			saint_first_of_faith_trigger = yes
		}
		saint_mark_faith_effect = yes
		random_realm_province = {
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { trait = i_used_to_be_a_player } }
				province_event = { id = saint.11 days = 365 random = 730 }
			}
			else = { province_event = { id = saint.11 days = 1095 random = 9855 } }
		}
	}
}

# Tells the dead one's realm and kin that they were named Pious (saint.3).
# Scope: the proclaimer; FROM of saint.3 is this character.
# Used by: WHG_saint_events
saint_news_pious_effect = {
	any_playable_ruler = {
		limit = {
			religion_group = event_target:saint_person
			same_realm = event_target:saint_top_liege
		}
		narrative_event = { id = saint.3 }
		set_character_flag = saint_news_sent
	}
	event_target:saint_person = {
		any_dynasty_member = {
			limit = {
				is_ruler = yes
				religion_group = event_target:saint_person
				NOT = { has_character_flag = saint_news_sent }
			}
			narrative_event = { id = saint.3 }
		}
	}
	saint_news_clear_effect = yes
}

# Tells every ruler of the faith and the dead one's kin that a Venerated
# Soul was named (saint.15). The head of the cult gets his own copy.
# Scope: the head of the cult (saint.12).
# Used by: WHG_saint_events
saint_news_venerated_effect = {
	any_playable_ruler = {
		limit = { religion = event_target:saint_person NOT = { character = ROOT } }
		narrative_event = { id = saint.15 }
		set_character_flag = saint_news_sent
	}
	event_target:saint_person = {
		any_dynasty_member = {
			limit = {
				is_ruler = yes
				religion_group = event_target:saint_person
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_character_flag = saint_news_sent }
			}
			narrative_event = { id = saint.15 }
		}
	}
	saint_news_clear_effect = yes
}

# Picks the miracle told of the new Venerated Soul and the name it earns.
# The miracle is stored on the soul for [saint_person.RandomSaintMiracle].
# Scope: the head of the cult (saint.12).
# Used by: WHG_saint_events
saint_miracle_effect = {
	event_target:saint_person = {
		random_list = {
			100 = { # The comet's mark on the palms
				set_variable = { which = global_miracle value = 1 }
				give_nickname = nick_the_god_bearing
			}
			100 = { # The body does not rot
				set_variable = { which = global_miracle value = 2 }
				give_nickname = nick_the_myrrh_streaming
			}
			800 = { # Healing
				trigger = { trait = physician }
				set_variable = { which = global_miracle value = 3 }
				if = { limit = { trait = charitable } give_nickname = nick_the_unmercenary_healer }
				else = { give_nickname = nick_the_healer }
			}
			200 = { # Prophecy
				trigger = { OR = { trait = mystic trait = possessed } }
				set_variable = { which = global_miracle value = 4 }
				give_nickname = nick_the_prophet
			}
			100 = { # Reads hearts
				set_variable = { which = global_miracle value = 5 }
				give_nickname = nick_the_understanding
			}
			100 = { # Casts out daemons
				trigger = { trait = zealous }
				set_variable = { which = global_miracle value = 6 }
				give_nickname = nick_the_righteous
			}
			100 = { # Visions of the god
				set_variable = { which = global_miracle value = 7 }
				give_nickname = nick_the_apostle
			}
			50 = { # Walks on the river
				set_variable = { which = global_miracle value = 8 }
				give_nickname = nick_the_confessor
			}
			50 = { # Heard after death
				set_variable = { which = global_miracle value = 9 }
				give_nickname = nick_the_venerable
			}
			25 = { # Holy fire
				set_variable = { which = global_miracle value = 10 }
				give_nickname = nick_the_spark_of_god
			}
			50 = { # Lifts the fallen beam
				set_variable = { which = global_miracle value = 11 }
				give_nickname = nick_the_right_believing
			}
			25 = { # The martyr walks home
				trigger = { martyrs_death_trigger = yes }
				set_variable = { which = global_miracle value = 12 }
				give_nickname = nick_the_martyr
			}
			25 = { # The blessed well
				set_variable = { which = global_miracle value = 13 }
				give_nickname = nick_the_miracle_worker
			}
			25 = { # Drives out the ratmen
				trigger = { OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
				set_variable = { which = global_miracle value = 14 }
				give_nickname = nick_the_fool_for_christ
			}
			25 = { # The silent hound
				set_variable = { which = global_miracle value = 15 }
				give_nickname = nick_the_merciful
			}
			25 = { # Rises in prayer
				set_variable = { which = global_miracle value = 16 }
				give_nickname = nick_the_pious
			}
		}
	}
}

# Raises the tomb: in the soul's capital, else a free neighbour of it, else
# a demesne or realm province without a tomb.
# Scope: the head of the cult (saint.12).
# Used by: WHG_saint_events
saint_raise_tomb_effect = {
	event_target:saint_capital = { owner = { save_event_target_as = saint_capital_owner } }
	if = {
		limit = { event_target:saint_capital = { saint_no_tomb_trigger = yes } }
		event_target:saint_capital = { saint_tomb_here_effect = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:saint_capital = {
				any_neighbor_province = {
					owner = { character = event_target:saint_capital_owner }
					saint_no_tomb_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:saint_capital = {
			random_neighbor_province = {
				limit = {
					owner = { character = event_target:saint_capital_owner }
					saint_no_tomb_trigger = yes
				}
				saint_tomb_here_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { event_target:random_saint_demesne_province = { saint_no_tomb_trigger = yes } }
		event_target:random_saint_demesne_province = { saint_tomb_here_effect = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { event_target:random_saint_realm_province = { saint_no_tomb_trigger = yes } }
		event_target:random_saint_realm_province = { saint_tomb_here_effect = yes }
	}
}

# Founds the holy bloodline of a new Venerated Soul. The kind follows the
# soul's life: defenders for zealots, monastic for the celibate; the style
# follows the faith (the imperial cults, the old faith, the rest).
# Scope: the head of the cult (saint.12).
# Used by: WHG_saint_events
saint_found_bloodline_effect = {
	event_target:saint_person = {
		random_list = {
			20 = {
				trigger = { OR = { trait = zealous trait = holy_warrior } }
				saint_bloodline_defender_effect = yes
			}
			20 = {
				trigger = { OR = { trait = celibate trait = monk trait = nun } }
				saint_bloodline_monastic_effect = yes
			}
			5 = { saint_bloodline_cultural_effect = yes }
			5 = {
				modifier = { factor = 4 OR = { trait = zealous trait = holy_warrior } }
				saint_bloodline_inquisitor_effect = yes
			}
			5 = { saint_bloodline_theologian_effect = yes }
		}
		new_bloodline = {
			set_name = saintly_bloodline_name
			set_description = saintly_bloodline_description
		}
	}
}

# A ruler who sides with a corrupt Grand Theogonist may catch his vice.
# Scope: the ruler; FROM is the Grand Theogonist (saint.32).
# Used by: WHG_saint_events
saint_catch_vice_effect = {
	FROM = {
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = trait
			impaler = { ROOT = { add_trait = cruel } }
			cannibal_trait = { ROOT = { add_trait = cruel } }
			hedonist = { ROOT = { add_trait = gluttonous } }
			drunkard = { ROOT = { add_trait = gluttonous } }
			seducer = { ROOT = { add_trait = lustful } }
			seductress = { ROOT = { add_trait = lustful } }
			lustful = { ROOT = { add_trait = lustful } }
			lunatic_1 = {
				ROOT = {
					add_trait = lunatic_1
					set_variable = { which = lunatic_counter value = 5 }
				}
			}
			lunatic_2 = {
				ROOT = {
					add_trait = lunatic_2
					set_variable = { which = lunatic_counter value = 10 }
				}
			}
			lunatic_3 = {
				ROOT = {
					add_trait = lunatic_3
					set_variable = { which = lunatic_counter value = 20 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# The tomb is robbed: it becomes a plundered tomb and its lord hears of it.
# Scope: the thief (saint.43).
# Used by: WHG_saint_events
saint_plunder_tomb_effect = {
	event_target:target_burial_site = {
		remove_province_modifier = tomb_of_a_saint
		add_province_modifier = { name = pillaged_tomb_of_a_saint duration = -1 }
	}
	event_target:offended_burial_site_owner = { character_event = { id = saint.45 } }
}

			}
			add_trait = patient
		}

# Sets where the pilgrim walks (the north, the south, the heart of the Old
# World, or far lands) and sends him on his way (saint.61).
# Scope: the pilgrim (saint.60).
# Used by: WHG_saint_events
saint_pilgrim_setout_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:pilgrimage_destination = {
				OR = {
					region = world_old_world_kislev
					region = world_norsca
					region = world_old_world_empire_north
					region = world_karaz_ankor_northern_holds
				}
			}
		}
		set_character_flag = saint_pilgrim_north
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:pilgrimage_destination = {
				OR = {
					region = world_old_world_tilea
					region = world_old_world_estalia
					region = world_old_world_border_princes
					region = world_greater_araby
					region = world_badlands
					region = world_nehekhara
				}
			}
		}
		set_character_flag = saint_pilgrim_south
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:pilgrimage_destination = {
				OR = {
					region = world_old_world_empire
					region = world_old_world_bretonnia
					region = world_athel_loren
					region = world_albion
				}
			}
		}
		set_character_flag = saint_pilgrim_home
	}
	else = { set_character_flag = saint_pilgrim_far }
	character_event = { id = saint.61 days = 15 }
}

# The road goes on to the tomb (saint.75).
# Scope: the pilgrim.
# Used by: WHG_saint_events
saint_pilgrim_arrive_effect = { hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = saint.75 days = 15 } } }

# Done at the tomb; home in two months (saint.77).
# Scope: the pilgrim (saint.75).
# Used by: WHG_saint_events
saint_pilgrim_tomb_done_effect = {
	hidden_effect = {
		clr_character_flag = saint_tomb_crowded
		clr_character_flag = saint_tomb_quiet
		clr_character_flag = saint_tomb_forgotten
		character_event = { id = saint.77 days = 60 }
	}
}

# Ends a tomb pilgrimage and clears everything a pilgrimage sets.
# Scope: the pilgrim.
# Used by: WHG_saint_events, WHG_old_world_faith_events
saint_pilgrim_clear_effect = {
	remove_trait = on_pilgrimage
	clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
	clr_character_flag = preparing_pilgrimage
	clr_character_flag = activated_pilgrimage
	clr_character_flag = saint_pilgrim_north
	clr_character_flag = saint_pilgrim_south
	clr_character_flag = saint_pilgrim_home
	clr_character_flag = saint_pilgrim_far
	clr_character_flag = saint_tomb_crowded
	clr_character_flag = saint_tomb_quiet
	clr_character_flag = saint_tomb_forgotten
}

# Death on the road: in a fight, by a snake, or on a cult's altar. Those
# who cannot die are spared.
# Scope: the pilgrim.
# Used by: WHG_saint_events
saint_pilgrim_death_effect = {
	if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
	else = { death = { death_reason = death_battle } }
}

# Used by: WHG_saint_events
saint_pilgrim_snake_death_effect = {
	if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
	else = { death = { death_reason = death_accident_snakes } }
}

# Used by: WHG_saint_events
saint_pilgrim_cult_death_effect = {
	if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
	else = { death = { death_reason = death_sacrificed } }
}

# The Errantry War is lost. Scope: the master of the order (the
# southern_crusade_war CB); FROM is the defender.
# Used by: ka_cb_types
crusade_south_failure_effect = {
	narrative_event = { id = crusade.23 }
	FROM = { narrative_event = { id = crusade.23 } }
	any_playable_ruler = {
		limit = { NOR = { character = ROOT character = FROM } }
		narrative_event = { id = crusade.23 }
	}
}

# An Errantry War qualifies every knight who rides in it: each Knight Errant
# of the Knights of Bretonnia on the winning side, and in the winners'
# realms, becomes a Knight of the Realm. Scope: the attacker
# (southern_crusade_war on_success).
# Used by: ka_cb_types
crusade_south_knights_errant_effect = {
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = crusade_south_knights_errant_TT
		any_attacker = {
			if = {
				limit = { crusade_south_knight_errant_trigger = yes }
				crusade_south_raise_knight_effect = yes
			}
			any_realm_character = {
				limit = { crusade_south_knight_errant_trigger = yes }
				crusade_south_raise_knight_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# The diverted Errantry War is over, won or lost: clear its flags and let the
# knights sail for Araby again in a year or two. Scope: any.
# Used by: ka_cb_types
crusade_diverted_cleanup_effect = {
	any_character = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = diverted_errantry_attacker
				has_character_flag = diverted_errantry_target
				has_character_flag = diverted_errantry_creditor
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = diverted_errantry_attacker
		clr_character_flag = diverted_errantry_target
		clr_character_flag = diverted_errantry_creditor
	}
	c_miragliano = { clr_title_flag = diverted_errantry_port }
	c_remas = { clr_title_flag = diverted_errantry_port }
	c_luccini = { clr_title_flag = diverted_errantry_port }
	810 = { province_event = { id = crusade.13 days = 365 random = 365 } } # Couronne
}

# Strips every claim on a high seat of the cults from the pretenders of a
# beaten realm. Scope: the defeated ruler.
# Used by: wh_cb_types
coronation_strip_pretenders_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { coronation_cult_claim_trigger = yes }
		remove_claim = k_cult_of_sigmar
		remove_claim = k_cult_of_ulric
		remove_claim = k_cult_of_taal_and_rhya
		remove_claim = k_cult_of_morr
	}
	any_realm_character = {
		limit = { coronation_cult_claim_trigger = yes }
		remove_claim = k_cult_of_sigmar
		remove_claim = k_cult_of_ulric
		remove_claim = k_cult_of_taal_and_rhya
		remove_claim = k_cult_of_morr
		prestige = -100
	}
}

# The ancestors' answer to an offering: one random boon (or bane, or
# silence) for the aim asked (offering_aim_*), weighted by the offering made
# (offering_tier_*: blood, a life or a hoard of gold, a feast, a token). The
# answer comes as offering.10 - offering.14 after some months. Then the
# offering flags are cleared.
# Scope: the worshipper.
# Used by: WHG_ancestor_offering_events
offering_answer_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = offering_aim_health }
		random_list = {
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 has_character_flag = offering_tier_blood }
				modifier = { factor = 0.05 has_character_flag = offering_tier_life }
				modifier = { factor = 0.01 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }
				modifier = { factor = 0.01 has_character_flag = offering_tier_token }
				set_character_flag = offering_boon_blessing
				character_event = { id = offering.10 days = 30 random = 30 }
			}
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.15 has_character_flag = offering_tier_blood }
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 has_character_flag = offering_tier_life }
				modifier = { factor = 0.01 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }
				modifier = { factor = 0.01 has_character_flag = offering_tier_token }
				modifier = { factor = 0 is_strong_trigger = yes }
				set_character_flag = offering_boon_robust
				character_event = { id = offering.10 days = 300 random = 30 }
			}
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.3 has_character_flag = offering_tier_blood }
				modifier = { factor = 0.3 has_character_flag = offering_tier_life }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }
				modifier = { factor = 0.15 has_character_flag = offering_tier_token }
				set_character_flag = offering_boon_vigor
				character_event = { id = offering.10 days = 200 random = 30 }
			}
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.05 has_character_flag = offering_tier_blood }
				modifier = { factor = 0.05 has_character_flag = offering_tier_life }
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }
				modifier = { factor = 0.15 has_character_flag = offering_tier_token }
				set_character_flag = offering_boon_illness
				character_event = { id = offering.10 days = 150 random = 30 }
			}
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.35 has_character_flag = offering_tier_blood }
				modifier = { factor = 0.3 has_character_flag = offering_tier_life }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }
				modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = offering_tier_token }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = offering_aim_bonds }
		random_list = {
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 has_character_flag = offering_tier_blood }
				modifier = { factor = 0.05 has_character_flag = offering_tier_life }
				modifier = { factor = 0.01 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }
				modifier = { factor = 0.01 has_character_flag = offering_tier_token }
				set_character_flag = offering_boon_respected
				character_event = { id = offering.11 days = 350 random = 30 }
			}
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.15 has_character_flag = offering_tier_blood }
				modifier = { factor = 0.15 has_character_flag = offering_tier_life }
				modifier = { factor = 0.15 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }
				modifier = { factor = 0.05 has_character_flag = offering_tier_token }
				modifier = { factor = 0 is_no_females_race = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = celibate }
				set_character_flag = offering_boon_lover
				character_event = { id = offering.11 days = 100 random = 30 }
			}
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.15 has_character_flag = offering_tier_blood }
				modifier = { factor = 0.15 has_character_flag = offering_tier_life }
				modifier = { factor = 0.15 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }
				modifier = { factor = 0.05 has_character_flag = offering_tier_token }
				set_character_flag = offering_boon_friend
				character_event = { id = offering.11 days = 150 random = 30 }
			}
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 has_character_flag = offering_tier_blood }
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 has_character_flag = offering_tier_life }
				modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 has_character_flag = offering_tier_token }
				set_character_flag = offering_boon_listener
				character_event = { id = offering.11 days = 300 random = 30 }
			}
			100 = {

				modifier = { factor = 0.15 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }

	else_if = {

				modifier = { factor = 0.05 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }
				modifier = { factor = 0.01 has_character_flag = offering_tier_token }
				set_character_flag = offering_boon_levy
				character_event = { id = offering.12 days = 150 random = 30 }
			}
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_character_flag = offering_tier_blood }
				modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = offering_tier_life }
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }
				modifier = { factor = 0.05 has_character_flag = offering_tier_token }
				set_character_flag = offering_boon_wartech
				character_event = { id = offering.12 days = 100 random = 30 }
			}
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_character_flag = offering_tier_blood }
				modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = offering_tier_life }
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }

				modifier = { factor = 0 is_strong_trigger = yes }
				set_character_flag = offering_boon_robust
				character_event = { id = offering.10 days = 360 random = 30 }
			}
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 has_character_flag = offering_tier_blood }
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 has_character_flag = offering_tier_life }
				modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }
				modifier = { factor = 0.15 has_character_flag = offering_tier_token }
				modifier = { factor = 0 can_have_more_leadership_traits = no }

			}

				set_character_flag = offering_boon_maimed
				character_event = { id = offering.12 days = 100 random = 30 }

			100 = {

				modifier = { factor = 0.35 has_character_flag = offering_tier_life }
				modifier = { factor = 0.35 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }
				modifier = { factor = 0.35 has_character_flag = offering_tier_token }

		}

				character_event = { id = offering.13 days = 500 random = 30 }
			}
			100 = {

				modifier = { factor = 0.15 has_character_flag = offering_tier_life }
				modifier = { factor = 0.15 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }
				modifier = { factor = 0.05 has_character_flag = offering_tier_token }
				set_character_flag = offering_boon_ecotech
				character_event = { id = offering.13 days = 100 random = 30 }
			}
			100 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 has_character_flag = offering_tier_blood }
				modifier = { factor = 0.15 has_character_flag = offering_tier_life }
				modifier = { factor = 0.15 has_character_flag = offering_tier_feast }
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 has_character_flag = offering_tier_token }
				set_character_flag = offering_boon_bargainer
				character_event = { id = offering.13 days = 200 random = 30 }
			}
			100 = {

# Kills an offered life at the altar, by ROOT's hand. Killing close kin makes
# ROOT a kinslayer.
# Scope: the victim; ROOT is the worshipper.
# Used by: WHG_ancestor_offering_events
offering_kill_victim_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			dynasty = ROOT
			is_close_relative = ROOT
			ROOT = { NOT = { trait = kinslayer } }
		}
		ROOT = {
			add_trait = kinslayer
			character_event = { id = traitnote.287 }
		}
	}
	death = { death_reason = death_sacrificed_ancestors killer = ROOT }
}

# Mass conversion (HF baptism events): when a secret cult's leader converts
# openly,
# the counties where the cult hid in secret convert with them.
# The mod's secret cults are the Chaos cults; the vanilla secret religious
# societies are not used.
# Used by: WHG_baptism_events
root_secret_religion_provinces_convert_effect = {
	ROOT = {
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = society_member_of
			cult_of_khorne = {
				PREV = {
					any_demesne_province = {
						limit = { has_province_modifier = secret_khorne_community }
						religion = khorne
						remove_province_modifier = secret_khorne_community
					}
				}
			}
			cult_of_nurgle = {
				PREV = {
					any_demesne_province = {
						limit = { has_province_modifier = secret_nurgle_community }
						religion = nurgle
						remove_province_modifier = secret_nurgle_community
					}
				}
			}
			cult_of_slaanesh = {
				PREV = {
					any_demesne_province = {
						limit = { has_province_modifier = secret_slaanesh_community }
						religion = slaanesh
						remove_province_modifier = secret_slaanesh_community
					}
				}
			}
			cult_of_tzeentch = {
				PREV = {
					any_demesne_province = {
						limit = { has_province_modifier = secret_tzeentch_community }
						religion = tzeentch
						remove_province_modifier = secret_tzeentch_community
					}
				}
			}
			cult_of_undivided = {
				PREV = {
					any_demesne_province = {
						limit = { has_province_modifier = secret_undivided_community }
						religion = chaos
						remove_province_modifier = secret_undivided_community
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_cathay_grace_events
add_sympathy_for_prevs_open_religion_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			PREV = { religion_group = old_world_group }
			NOT = { religion_group = old_world_group }
		}
		add_trait = sympathy_old_world_group
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			PREV = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }
			NOT = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }
		}
		add_trait = sympathy_ormazd_gods_group
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			PREV = { religion_group = cadai_group }
			NOT = { religion_group = cadai_group }
		}
		add_trait = sympathy_cadai_group
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			PREV = { religion_group = dwarf_gods_group }
			NOT = { religion_group = dwarf_gods_group }
		}
		add_trait = sympathy_dwarf_gods_group
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			PREV = { religion_group = halfling_gods_group }
			NOT = { religion_group = halfling_gods_group }
		}
		add_trait = sympathy_halfling_gods_group
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			PREV = { religion_group = oriental_gods_group }
			NOT = { religion_group = oriental_gods_group }
		}
		add_trait = sympathy_oriental_gods_group
	}
}

# A token of the shrine (pilgrim.61): an ampulla for the Old World cults,
# an ember of Asuryan, an acorn of the Oak of Ages, a vial from the
# Cauldron of Blood or an ancestor stone.
# Scope: the pilgrim.
# Used by: WHG_character_effects, WHG_saint_events
pilgrim_token_effect = {
	set_character_flag = pilgrim_got_token
	if = {
		limit = { religion_group = old_world_group }
		add_artifact = pilgrim_ampulla
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = pilgrim_to_isle_of_flame }
		add_artifact = pilgrim_asuryan_ember
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = pilgrim_to_oak_of_ages }
		add_artifact = pilgrim_oak_acorn
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = pilgrim_to_har_ganeth }
		add_artifact = pilgrim_khaine_vial
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { religion_group = dwarf_gods_group }
		add_artifact = pilgrim_ancestor_stone
	}
}

# Sympathy for the faith a character is about to leave: the sympathy trait of
# his current religion group. Call it before the religion changes.
# Groups without a sympathy trait (Chaos, greenskins, the Horned Rat...) get
# nothing. Scope: the convert. Used by faith.601 and faith.700.
# Used by: WHG_faith_events
faith_old_faith_sympathy_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { religion_group = old_world_group }
		add_trait = sympathy_old_world_group
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }
		add_trait = sympathy_ormazd_gods_group
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { religion_group = cadai_group }
		add_trait = sympathy_cadai_group
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { religion_group = cytharai_group }
		add_trait = sympathy_cytharai_group
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { religion_group = dwarf_gods_group }
		add_trait = sympathy_dwarf_gods_group
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { religion_group = halfling_gods_group }
		add_trait = sympathy_halfling_gods_group
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { religion_group = oriental_gods_group }
		add_trait = sympathy_oriental_gods_group
	}
}

# A hunter makes a fine kill at the herdstone (herdhunt.22); so may one with
# no calling yet (25%).
# Scope: the Beastlord or a chieftain.
# Used by: WHG_great_hunt_events
herdhunt_shine_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { trait = hunter }
		character_event = { id = herdhunt.22 days = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { lifestyle_traits = 1 } }
		random = {
			chance = 25
			character_event = { id = herdhunt.22 days = 2 }
		}
	}
}

# Sympathy for the faith group of the band's lord (merc.305).
# Scope: the captain; event_target:merc_faith_lord the lord.
# Used by: WHG_mercenary_events

		limit = { event_target:merc_faith_lord = { religion_group = old_world_group } }
		add_trait = sympathy_old_world_group
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { event_target:merc_faith_lord = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group } }
		add_trait = sympathy_ormazd_gods_group
	}
	else_if = {

		add_trait = sympathy_oriental_gods_group
	}
}

# The taint is burned out: the flags, possession and the daemons' voices go.
# Scope: the child.
# Used by: WHG_seeress_events
seeress_cleanse_child_effect = {
	seeress_clear_child_flags_effect = yes
	remove_character_modifier = voice_of_greater_daemon
	remove_character_modifier = voice_of_tzeentch
	remove_character_modifier = holy_comet
	remove_character_modifier = lycanthropy
	if = {
		limit = { trait = possessed }
		remove_trait = possessed
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_sway_events
random_antagonizer_slander_roll_effect = {
	random_list = {
		75 = { #Nothing
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.75
				FROM = {
					attribute_diff = { character = ROOT attribute = intrigue  value = 4 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.75
				FROM = {
					attribute_diff = { character = ROOT attribute = intrigue  value = 6 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = {
					attribute_diff = { character = ROOT attribute = intrigue  value = 8 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = {
					attribute_diff = { character = ROOT attribute = intrigue  value = 10 }
				}
			}
		}
		25 = { #Bonus event
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				NOR = {
					trait = stressed
					trait = depressed
				}
				opinion = { who = FROM value = 20 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				FROM = {
					has_character_flag = flag_antagonize_slander_generic
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				FROM = {
					intrigue = 15
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				FROM = {
					intrigue = 18
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				FROM = {
					intrigue = 21
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				FROM = {
					intrigue = 25
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.75
				FROM = {
					intrigue = 30
				}
			}
			random_antagonizer_slander_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_warrior_society_events
died_founding_a_bloodline_effect = { #for the main heir
	#send news of death to other heroes/society members?
	save_event_target_as = bloodline_founder
	found_a_legendary_bloodline_effect = yes

	if = {
        limit = { ai = no }
        #Adding flag to player and new player char, for safety...
        set_character_flag = achievement_zero_to_hero
        current_heir = { set_character_flag = achievement_zero_to_hero }

        if = {
        	limit = { current_heir = { NOT = { dynasty = PREV } } } # in case of game over...
        	set_global_flag = achievement_zero_to_hero
        }
    }

	current_heir = {
		save_event_target_as = new_story_owner
		character_event = { id = lodge.741 days = 1 } #send event of what happened...
	}
	any_society_member = {
        limit = {
            same_society_as = ROOT
            NOT = { character = ROOT }
            current_heir = { NOT = { character = PREV } }
        }
        narrative_event = { id = lodge.742 days = 3 } #News event to fellow society members...
    }
}

# Used by: WHG_warrior_society_events
found_a_bloodline_effect = { #for alive hero
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_killed_at_least_1_trigger = yes
			has_killed_at_least_3_trigger = no
		}
		set_character_flag = bloodline_quality_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_killed_at_least_3_trigger = yes
			has_killed_at_least_5_trigger = no
		}
		set_character_flag = bloodline_quality_2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_killed_at_least_5_trigger = yes
		}
		set_character_flag = bloodline_quality_3
	}
	save_event_target_as = bloodline_founder
	narrative_event = { id = lodge.740 days = 3 }
	clr_XYZ_desc_flag_effect = yes
}
